2026-09-05T09:59:42Z  FINDING-AGAINST-ME  I got the mechanism wrong and passed it to my child. My 09:51 journal and NOTE-002 said the sibling filter rejects "names beginning B or T" and singled out TraceDriverV1 for starting with T. B and T are the BUILT-MODULE and THEOREM SET names in the S2 contract — set labels, not first letters. The actual rule is a namespace-prefix allowlist of Reactivegas and KelGroups.
